    function _SafeMul(uint256 varg0, uint256 varg1) private {
        require((varg0 * varg1 / varg0 == varg1) | !varg0, Panic(17)); // arithmetic overflow or underflow
        return varg0 * varg1;
    }

    function fun_locateCurrentAmount(uint256 varg0, uint256 varg1, uint256 varg2, uint256 varg3) private {
        if (varg0 - varg1) {
            v0 = _SafeMul(varg0, varg3 - varg2 - (block.timestamp - varg2));
            v1 = _SafeMul(varg1, block.timestamp - varg2);
            require(v0 <= v0 + v1, Panic(17)); // arithmetic overflow or underflow
            return bool(v0 + v1) * ((v0 + v1) / (varg3 - varg2));
        } else {
            return varg1;
        }
    }

    function fun_locateCurrentAmount_20585(uint256 varg0, uint256 varg1, uint256 varg2, uint256 varg3) private {

        }
    }

    function fun_getFraction(uint256 varg0, uint256 varg1, uint256 varg2) private {
        if (varg0 == varg1) {

        } else {
            require(!(varg2 * varg0 % varg1), InexactFraction());
            v0 = _SafeMul(varg2, varg0);
